Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the optics structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 617. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 665 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the optics decomposition. By minimality, the element a^617 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 665-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
